# Asset manifest

Every non-code asset GregGuns needs, derived from the design records ([0001](../design/0001-tier-map.md), [0002](../design/0002-machine-deconfliction.md), [0006](../design/0006-art-direction.md), [0009](../design/0009-gtnh-integration-defaults.md), [0010](../design/0010-material-remap-and-tier-additions.md), [0011](../design/0011-machine-policy.md)), the spec sheets and the measured Techguns footprints in [ASSET-FACTS.md](../techguns-spec/ASSET-FACTS.md) and [AUDIO-PROFILES.md](../techguns-spec/AUDIO-PROFILES.md).

The machine-readable twin is [tools/asset-gen/manifest.json](../../tools/asset-gen/manifest.json), built by `python -I tools/asset-gen/build_manifest.py`. Edit the script, not the generated files.

**Totals:** 821 assets. By kind: icon 177, model 165, block 128, gui 14, particle 31, sound 279, branding 11, infographic 16. By priority: P1 34, P2 313, P3 474.

## Conventions

- **Target paths** are under `src/main/resources/assets/gregguns/` unless they start with `docs/assets/`.
- **Techguns footprint** is a path in the Techguns resource tree used only for its size, frame count, color or duration. Nothing is copied from it ([CONTRIBUTING § Originality](../../CONTRIBUTING.md#originality-the-techguns-boundary)).
- **Item icons** are 16x16 GT-style ([0006](../design/0006-art-direction.md)). Palettes come from the GT material color (`MaterialsInit.java`, 5.09.54.205); items made of a material use that color. Rubber is `#000000` in GT: paint it as a lifted dark gray (`#2A2A2E`) so it reads.
- **Model textures** keep the Techguns size and UV layout. **Decorative block textures** keep the Techguns footprint (32 px, connected-texture sheets 64 px). **Machine overlays and multiblock casings** are GT-native 16 px; animated ones are vertical strips with a `.png.mcmeta` (`frametime` in ticks).
- **Sounds** match role, length and loudness: integrated loudness within +/-3 LU of the measured original (`target_lufs`), peak at or below -1 dBFS, positional sounds mono (GVA-23). Delivery rate is 44.1 kHz, or 48 kHz where the original was 48 kHz. Stereo only for the glider loop (player-bound ambience).
- **Priority:** **P1** is the M1 vertical slice (9mm Pistol, its ammo and magazine, its sounds, the LV Ammo Press and its HUD/GUI). **P2** is M2 to M4 content (ballistic arsenal, armor, production chain, branding basics). **P3** is M5 onwards (energy and exotic weapons, utility and defense, NPCs, worldgen), camo and color variants, social and infographic art.
- Provenance for every delivered asset goes in [CREDITS.md](CREDITS.md).

## 10. Excluded on purpose

Not in the manifest because GregGuns dropped them or GT supplies them.

- **Ores, ore clusters, oil, fluids** (`oreCluster*`, `oreTitanIron`, `oreUranium`, `ore*Block`, `acid_*`, `milk_*`, creeper acid bucket): 0002/0005, GT owns ores and fluids.
- **Techguns materials** (ingots, nuggets, plates, wires, circuit boards, rubber, plastic, carbon fibers, coil, ore items, obsidian steel): 0002, GT materials.
- **Dropped machines** (Metal Press, Chem Lab, Fabricator, Charging Station, Ore Drill, Scrapper): their block sheets, ore-drill frame/rod/head textures, GUIs and work sounds. Machine upgrade item (`machineStackUpgrade`) and the Techguns `paintmachine_*` / legacy `rc_*` block files.
- **Energy cell** (`cell_energy`, `energyCell*`): 0010. **Jetpack** (item, worn texture and four sounds): 0009. **Electric engine** item: GT motors.
- **Debug and authoring tools** (`blockReaderTool`, `structureScanTool`, `structureTestTool`, `oreClusterScanner`, `tgx`, `testItem`) and `TGPlayerInventory` as a tab GUI (Baubles Expanded replaces it; only the HUD sprites are kept, as `gui.hud`).
- **Unused Techguns files** (listed unused in the area texture sheets): `pistol.png`, `pistol2Texture.png`, `mp5Texture.png`, `neutrinogun.png`, `drillTexture.png`, old-style plates, unused fx sheets (laserflare01, laserFlash2x2, lightning*, lensflare2/4, heatray2, glow, blood01, fireball*), `tent`, `flagBlock`, `steelframe_glass`, `Bioblock`.
- **Sounds dropped:** `test.ogg`, four files in no `sounds.json` event (`beamgun_reload`, `chainsaw_loop_d`, `minigun_start_b/c`), the mono `glider_loop.ogg` (the stereo loop is kept), jetpack and dropped-machine sounds (41 files skipped).
- **Vanilla-textured mobs:** Skeleton Soldier and Pigman Soldier use vanilla skins and need no art.

## 11. Notes and non-image data

- **Structures (11-worldgen)** need no textures. Structure templates, loot tables and `sounds.json`/lang files are data, written alongside the code (structure files are written fresh, never copied).
- **Sounds not in this list:** Rampart, Gantry and Ammunition Works have no sound brief yet ([ammo-press.md § Sound](../gregguns-spec/08-machines-production/ammo-press.md#sound) asks only for the stroke hook). The Ammo Press stroke reuses the `ammopress_work2` role.
- **Armored-turret variants** use the five Techguns turret sheets; the obsidian-steel variant becomes aluminum (0010).
- **Recolor rule:** one gun sheet serves its akimbo form; the Flashbang recolors the Frag Grenade sheet.
